uint64\n33\t\tMSpanInuse      uint64\n34\t\tMSpanSys        uint64\n35\t\tMCacheInuse     uint64\n36\t\tMCacheSys       uint64\n37\t\tBuckHashSys     uint64\n38\t\tGCSys           uint64\n39\t\tNextGC          uint64\n40\t\tGoroutines      int\n41\t\tNumGC           uint32\n42\t\tGCPauseLast     time.Duration\n43\t\tSys             uint64\n44\t\tTotalAlloc      uint64\n45\t\tNumForcedGC     uint32\n46\t\tGCCPUFraction   float64\n47\t\tIPLimiterCount  int\n48\t\tCacheEntries    int\n49\t}\n50\t\n51\t// countIPLimiters returns the total number of IP limiter entries across all limiters.\n52\t// Must be called within a test that has access to the package-internal fields.\n53\tfunc countIPLimiters(rl *RateLimiter) int {\n54\t\trl.webLimit.mu.RLock()\n55\t\twebCount := len(rl.webLimit.limiters)\n56\t\trl.webLimit.mu.RUnlock()\n57\t\n58\t\trl.apiLimit.mu.RLock()\n59\t\tapiCount := len(rl.apiLimit.limiters)\n60\t\trl.apiLimit.mu.RUnlock()\n61\t\n62\t\trl.bulkLimit.mu.RLock()\n63\t\tbulkCount := len(rl.bulkLimit.limiters)\n64\t\trl.bulkLimit.mu.RUnlock()\n65\t\n66\t\treturn webCount + apiCount + bulkCount\n67\t}\n68\t\n69\tfunc takeSnapshot(t *testing.T, rl *RateLimiter, cache *checker.ResultCache) memSnapshot {\n70\t\tt.Helper()\n71\t\tvar m runtime.MemStats\n72\t\truntime.GC()\n73\t\ttime.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond) // let GC settle\n74\t\truntime.ReadMemStats(&m)\n75\t\n76\t\tvar cacheLen int\n77\t\tif cache != nil {\n78\t\t\tcacheLen = cache.Len()\n79\t\t}\n80\t\n81\t\treturn memSnapshot{\n82\t\t\tTimestamp:      time.Now(),\n83\t\t\tHeapAlloc:      m.HeapAlloc,\n84\t\t\tHeapSys:        m.HeapSys,\n85\t\t\tHeapInuse:      m.HeapInuse,\n86\t\t\tHeapIdle:       m.HeapIdle,\n87\t\t\tHeapReleased:   m.HeapReleased,\n88\t\t\tHeapObjects:    m.HeapObjects,\n89\t\t\tStackInuse:     m.StackInuse,\n90\t\t\tStackSys:       m.StackSys,\n91\t\t\tMSpanInuse:     m.MSpanInuse,\n92\t\t\tMSpanSys:       m.MSpanSys,\n93\t\t\tMCacheInuse:    m.MCacheInuse,\n94\t\t\tMCacheSys:      m.MCacheSys,\n95\t\t\tBuckHashSys:    m.BuckHashSys,\n96\t\t\tGCSys:          m.GCSys,\n97\t\t\tNextGC:         m.NextGC,\n98\t\t\tGoroutines:     runtime.NumGoroutine(),\n99\t\t\tNumGC:          m.NumGC,\n100\t\t\tGCPauseLast:    time.Duration(m.PauseNs[(m.NumGC+255)%256]),\n101\t\t\tSys:            m.Sys,\n102\t\t\tTotalAlloc:     m.TotalAlloc,\n103\t\t\tNumForcedGC:    m.NumForcedGC,\n104\t\t\tGCCPUFraction:  m.GCCPUFraction,\n105\t\t\tIPLimiterCount: countIPLimiters(rl),\n106\t\t\tCacheEntries:   cacheLen,\n107\t\t}\n108\t}\n109\t\n110\t// bytesMB converts bytes to megabytes with 2 decimal places.\n111\tfunc bytesMB(b uint64) float64 {\n112\t\treturn float64(b) / (1024 * 1024)\n113\t}\n114\t\n115\t// memoryTestChecker is a lightweight mock DomainChecker + BulkChecker for load tests.\n116\ttype memoryTestChecker struct{}\n117\t\n118\tfunc (mc *memoryTestChecker) Check(_ context.Context, normalizedDomain string) (*domain.DomainResult, error) {\n119\t\treturn \"com\",\n136\t\t\t\tSource:    \"mock\",\n137\t\t\t}\n138\t\t}\n139\t\treturn result\n140\t}\n141\t\n142\t// TestMemoryGrowthUnderLoad runs 50 req/s for 30 seconds and verifies memory growth\n143\t// {\n274\t\t\t\tdefer wg.Done()\n275\t\t\t\t<-started\n276\t\t\t\tclient := &http.Client{\n277\t\t\t\t\tTimeout: 5 * time.Second,\n278\t\t\t\t\tTransport: trends.\n346\t\tt.Log(\"\\n=== Memory Growth Analysis ===\")\n347\t\tif len(snapshots) > 2 {\n348\t\t\tmid := len(snapshots) / 2\n349\t\t\tvar firstHalfSum, secondHalfSum uint64\n350\t\t\tfor i, s := 1)\n378\t\t\t\tt.Logf(\"Final %d snapshots avg per-interval growth: %.2f MB\", lastN, lastNSlope)\n379\t\t\t}\n380\t\t}\n381\t\n382\t\t// Assertions.\n383\t\tt.Run(\"heap_growth\", func(t *testing.T) {\n384\t\t\tif heapGrowthMB > 100 {\n385\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"Heap grew %.2f MB (> 100 MB threshold) — possible memory leak\", heapGrowthMB)\n386\t\t\t} else {\n387\t\t\t\tt.Logf(\"PASS: Heap grew %.2f TestIPLimiterCleanup verifies that stale IP limiter entries are cleaned up.\n422\tfunc TestIPLimiterCleanup(t *testing.T) {\n423\t\trl := NewRateLimiter(testLogger(t))\n424\t\n425\t\t// Simulate 1000 unique IPs hitting the API endpoint.\n426\t\tfor i := 0; i < 1000; i++ {\n427\t\t\tip := fmt.Sprintf(\"10.1.%d.%d\", i/256, i%256)\n428\t\t\tlimiter := custom limiter.\n439\t\ttestIPL := newIPLimiter(1000, 5) // 1000 req/min ≈ 16.7/sec, burst 5\n440\t\ttestIP := \"192.168.1.1\"\n441\t\tlim := time.Millisecond)\n455\t\ttestIPL.cleanup()\n456\t\tif _, exists := testIPL.limiters[testIP]; exists {\n457\t\t\tt.Log(\"Note: IP still present after cleanup (tokens may not be fully replenished {\n465\t\t\tt.Logf(\"IP limiter still present after 2.5s — tokens=%.1f (burst=%d)\",\n466\t\t\t\ttestIPL.limiters[testIP].Tokens(), testIPL.burst)\n467\t\t}\n468\t}\n469\t\n470\t// TestIPLimiterMemoryGrowth measures memory usage of the ipLimiter map under load.\n471\tfunc TestIPLimiterMemoryGrowth(t *testing.T) {\n472\t\trl := NewRateLimiter(testLogger(t))\n473\t\n474\t\truntime.GC()\n475\t\tvar m1 runtime.MemStats\n476\t\truntime.ReadMemStats(&m1)\n477\t\tbaseHeap := m1.HeapAlloc\n478\t\n479\t\t// Add 50000 unique IP i%256)\n482\t\t\trl.apiLimit.getLimiter(ip)\n483\t\t}\n484\t\n485\t\truntime.GC()\n486\t\tvar m2 runtime.MemStats\n487\t\truntime.ReadMemStats(&m2)\n488\t\n489\t\tgrowth := m2.HeapAlloc -
